### 2026-08-23 (Sun) MIDDAY — **THE DAY THE ROLE CHANGED: a design session with zero dispatches, and two defects found in my own instruments**

**② The paradigm shifted under the desk.** PROME **RULED the dark-owner doorbell ADOPTED** (3 amendments + 4 riders), landed rule 6b and the root mirror, then encoded and launched the **two-tier orchestration pilot**. My top carried item flipped from *waiting on PROME* to *owed by me*.

**③ TERRY refuted my 30Y framing, and it was right.** *"Exactly round-tripped ⇒ 9 Sep is UN-PRICED"* does not survive: `DGS30` ends 8/20 (I verified at FRED myself — there is no 8/21 official, my fourth point was a cash index), the measured `^TYX`−`DGS30` basis is +0.39bp mean over n=11 so 8/21 estimates to ≈5.27 (**~8 of 9bp, not "exactly"**), and **the LEVEL was the wrong instrument** — the 10Y round-tripped further, so the whole curve came back. **④ Will directed the retune** (*"adjust your role and guidelines to fit the new paradigm"*), overriding the hold I had put on myself under base-rate-before-wiring.

🔑 **THE ARGUMENT, and it is the session's durable idea: the gate did not become wrong — the PRICE BEHIND IT FELL.** The remedy went from *waking a whole desk in its own window* to *a bounded subagent touch that drains the whole inbox*. **A gate calibrated against the old price is systematically tight, and its error costs INVERT: an over-doorbell wastes something cheap and is loud; an under-doorbell is silent.** ⇒ **the unit of decision moved from the ITEM to the DESK** (action item = trigger, backlog = yield), a **P0** precondition was added for the new third desk-state, and **leg 3b (cadence-is-the-deadline)** was added — the first limb that can reach the no-clock backlog desks **the ruling itself had named** in its own §4. Old gate **1-of-7** on the 8/22 night, unable to reach HENRY or BROCK **by construction**; new gate **≈3-of-7**, and the two adds are exactly those desks.

**⑤ Shipped:** `BOARD_CONSUMPTION_SPEC` **v0.20 §3.5.7** · `CHECKLIST` **v0.37** Phase 3.5 · `CLAUDE.md` IDENTITY **fourth question** + RULE 13 + boot step **9b** · `registry/DOORBELL_LOG.tsv` · `STATE.md` §1 swept · rule **6b amended** under Will's in-session permission (recorded in the amendment as an **authorised exception, not a precedent**) · packet + rule-6 doorbell to PROME. **The role, stated once: Routing v2 closed *"in BOARD ≠ received"*; this closes *"received ≠ RECEIVABLE."* WALTER is a router PLUS a readiness monitor for the receiving layer — and still never spawns.**

**⑥ 🔴 P0 BROKE AND WAS FIXED BEFORE FIRST USE — and I found it by asking the question that broke it in the same packet that shipped it.** PROME's answer: `ORCH_LOG`'s `IN-FLIGHT` updates at PROME's *consumption*, lagging a real delivery by minutes and **a DEATH indefinitely**. Uncorrected, P0 would have **suppressed the doorbell for a desk that died mid-touch — the case needing it most**: the exact silent false-negative the mechanism exists to remove, reintroduced by its own precondition. **Operative form: `IN-FLIGHT` never suppresses alone; P0 fails only when corroborated by `ListAgents` presence or recent desk-dir writes.** Convergent with PROME's own failure-path STEP 0 (~11:0x vs my ~10:5x, neither having seen the other); PROME adopted my desk-dir-recency limb with credit and encoded the semantics in the ledger header at `f1c33ff1f`.

**⑨ 🔴 THEN WILL RE-OPENED IT: *"what is the backlog?"* — and the second half is the more valuable one.** First clarification: **124 is DELIVERY SLIPS, not signals — 88 distinct signals, 21 with an action owner.** Then the real question, *"are you sure these have not been consumed?"*, and it was right: the figure rests on **"nobody moved a file," a PROXY.** Built the second instrument — `delivered_but_unconsumed` now reads the **recipient's own `board_log.tsv`** — splitting 124 into **92 CORROBORATED** (two independent records agree) and **32 UNTESTABLE** (8 ACTION) at five desks keeping no consumption record: ZHAO 18 · OTTO 7 · HANS 5 · WATT 1 · DEWEY 1. ⚠️ **The cross-check fires ZERO times, and the zero IS the result** — it is what upgrades the 92 from inferred to corroborated. **⑩ 🔴 THE SCRATCH QUERY I WROTE TO ANSWER WILL WAS ITSELF THE DEFECT — and I briefly blamed the telemetry for it.** It tested whether `delivery_log`'s RECORDED path exists; for a consumed item that path already points into `processed/`. **⑪ THE EXPIRED SWEEP: ZERO QUALIFIED, NOTHING SWEPT.** Will's *"be careful about what you consider retired"* earned its place immediately. §3.7's test is *"the dated decision has PASSED,"* not *"it is old."* **Both retirements I had proposed were wrong and both erred toward making the number smaller:** the oldest was **already marked EXPIRED on 8/7**, and HENRY's 16d item — the strongest-looking candidate — **corrects the MU resolver in FOUR dispatched signals against a date five weeks in the FUTURE.** Retiring it would have deleted a live correction sitting upstream of four others.

**⑫ ✅ PACKET → PROME, ADOPTED IN ~30 MINUTES.** Root cause was a **stalled Phase-2 rollout**, not a defect: SPEC §8 has WALTER define the consume boot-step and never edit recipients' boot docs, recipients self-apply, and the telemetry is the time-box — **19 of 45 registry agents keep a `board_log`. ## ACTIVE DESIGN WORK — Apr 7 COP architecture narrative (archived 2026-05-04)

*This section captures the original COP architectural exploration from Apr 7. Decisions referenced here were captured into COP.md / CLAUDE.md / design specs as those layers shipped. Kept here for design history; not boot-relevant.*

**COP Architecture (in progress with Will)**

Exploring a shift from point-to-point inbox messaging to a Common Operating Picture model. Key decisions from Apr 7 session:

1. **Three-layer hybrid model proposed:**
   - Layer 1: COP directory with structured layers (BLUE, RED_FORCE, SIGNALS, POSITIONS, CATALYSTS, SUMMARY) — the "open" shared view
   - Layer 2: Signal archive (WALTER/signals/) — all signals in one place, not copied to N inboxes
   - Layer 3: Push notifications — lightweight inbox pings for FLASH/IMMEDIATE only

2. **Models researched:** Military COP, Blackboard Pattern, IC Dissemination ("discover broadly, retrieve selectively"), Event-Driven Patterns (Kafka), Wire Service (AP/Reuters), Cognitive Load research

3. **Key insight:** Pure "everything in the open" fails due to cognitive overload. COP must be CURATED (editorial judgment), not a data dump. WALTER = J3 (integrator), not just a router.

4. **Open questions:**
   - Layer architecture details (which layers, update cadence)
   - How this interacts with Prome's coordination role
   - Whether inbox system evolves or gets replaced
   - How agents change boot sequence to read COP
   - Who maintains the COP when WALTER isn't in session (Prome backup?)
   - Real-time watching via file watchers / Prome polling vs ephemeral agent sessions

5. **Will's direction:** Treat this as a living process, iterate as we learn. Don't over-design in isolation.
